with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems); iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":261,"totalLines":261,"truncated":false}},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/spmv_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r5/spmv/spmv.cpp","time":{"start":1792691277848,"end":1792691277966}},"id":"prt_164ba0fdd0012VZCqZtxJZmsoW","sessionID":"ses_e9b46040fffeLOogkE6LXv8fui","messageID":"msg_164ba0939001cRrhMOcZU77bWw"}}

The threads of a group stride over the non-zeroes of\n// its row, which keeps accesses to val/cols coalesced, and the partial sums\n// are reduced with warp shuffles. When UseShared is set, the dense vector is\n// staged in shared memory so the random gathers hit on-chip storage.\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int ThreadsPerRow, bool UseShared>\n__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec,\n                              const index_t dim,\n                              double* __restrict__ out) {\n    extern __shared__ double svec[];\n    if constexpr (UseShared) {\n        for (index_t i = threadIdx.x; i < dim; i += blockDim.x) {\n            svec[i] = vec[i];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    const unsigned long long tid =\n        static_cast<unsigned long long>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const unsigned long long row64 = tid / ThreadsPerRow;\n    const bool active = row64 < dim;\n\n    unsigned int ballot = 0xffffffffu;\n    if constexpr (ThreadsPerRow > 1) {\n        ballot = __ballot_sync(0xffffffffu, active);\n    }\n    if (!active) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(row64);\n    const index_t lane = static_cast<index_t>(tid % ThreadsPerRow);\n    const index_t start = rowDelimiters[row];\n    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    if constexpr (UseShared) {\n        for (index_t j = start + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {\n            sum += val[j] * svec[cols[j]];\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (index_t j = start + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {\n            sum += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if constexpr (ThreadsPerRow > 1) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            sum += __shfl_down_sync(ballot, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);\n        }\n    }\n    if (lane == 0) {\n        out[row] = sum;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: launchSpMV\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Selects a thread-per-row granularity that matches the average number of\n//   non-zeroes per row and launches the CUDA kernel on the default stream.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int ThreadsPerRow>\nvoid launchSpMVKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n                      const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out, bool useShared,\n                      int blockSize, unsigned int gridSize) {\n    const size_t sharedBytes = useShared ? static_cast<size_t>(dim) * sizeof(double) : 0;\n    if (useShared) {\n        spmvCsrKernel<ThreadsPerRow, true><<<gridSize, blockSize, sharedBytes>>>(\n            val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);\n    } else {\n        spmvCsrKernel<ThreadsPerRow, false><<<gridSize, blockSize, 0>>>(\n            val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid launchSpMV(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n                const double* vec, const index_t dim, const index_t nnz, double* out) {\n    if (dim == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Pick power-of-two threads per row up to a full warp based on the average\n    // row length. Short rows use fewer cooperating threads; long rows use a\n    // warp per row.\n    const index_t avgRowLength = nnz / dim;\n    int threadsPerRow = 1;\n    while (threadsPerRow < 32 && static_cast<index_t>(threadsPerRow * 2) <= avgRowLength) {\n        threadsPerRow <<= 1;\n    }\n\n    // The dense vector fits in shared memory for reasonably sized problems.\n    const bool useShared = (static_cast<size_t>(dim) * sizeof(double)) <= 32u * 1024u;\n    const int blockSize = 256;\n    const unsigned long long totalThreads =\n        static_cast<unsigned long long>(dim) * static_cast<unsigned long long>(threadsPerRow);\n    const unsigned int gridSize =\n        static_cast<unsigned int>((totalThreads + blockSize - 1) / blockSize);\n\n    switch (threadsPerRow) {\n        case 1:\n            launchSpMVKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, useShared, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;\n        case 2:\n            launchSpMVKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, useShared, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;\n        case 4:\n            launchSpMVKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, useShared, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            launchSpMVKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, useShared, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;","lineStart":120,"lineEnd":239,"totalLines":443,"truncated":true}},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/spmv_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r5/spmv/spmv.cpp","time":{"start":1792691722234,"end":1792691722248}},"id":"prt_164c0d7c600114F2so5XXLrh4D","sessionID":"ses_e9b46040fffeLOogkE6LXv8fui","messageID":"msg_164c0b02c001DDBbnPBvhvpHiV"}}

1) / blockSize);\n\n    switch (threadsPerRow) {\n        case 1:\n            launchSpMVKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;\n        case 2:\n            launchSpMVKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;\n        case 4:\n            launchSpMVKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            launchSpMVKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            launchSpMVKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;\n        default:\n            launchSpMVKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out, blockSize, gridSize);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector","lineStart":115,"lineEnd":234,"totalLines":425,"truncated":true}},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/spmv_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r5/spmv/spmv.cpp","time":{"start":1792692252975,"end":1792692252992}},"id":"prt_164c8f0fe0015oRD5dEBrVjqkc","sessionID":"ses_e9b46040fffeLOogkE6LXv8fui","messageID":"msg_164c8cf640014SJWgGm9AgDcUy"}}
